# Asset manifest

Generated from the JSON reference manifest, all archived fact-sheet HTML, CSS `url(...)` references, the live loaded-player DOM, and the GWT bootstrap. The machine-readable equivalent is `data/manifests/assets.json`. “Provenance-sensitive” is a review flag, not a legal conclusion.
